(m) — bottom, far end, or back of a space.<br><br><strong>Key phrases:</strong><div>• <strong>ao fundo (de)</strong> = at the end of / at the back of: <em>ao fundo da rua</em> (at the end of the street).</div><div>• <strong>no fundo</strong> = deep down / actually: <em>no fundo, ele é simpático</em> (deep down, he's nice).</div><div>• <strong>a fundo</strong> = thoroughly: <em>estudar a fundo</em> (to study thoroughly).</div><div>• <strong>de fundo</strong> = underlying / basic: <em>um problema de fundo</em> (an underlying problem).</div><br><strong>As adjective — <em>fundo / funda</em></strong> = deep: <em>um rio fundo</em> (a deep river), <em>uma gaveta funda</em> (a deep drawer).<br><br><strong>Financial sense</strong> (B1+): <em>um fundo (de investimento)</em> = an (investment) fund. <em>Fundos públicos</em> = public funds.
